! 0x870–0x8A8: Signed string-to-integer conversion
! Steps:
! 1. Create a stack frame.
! 2. Read the first character and check for '-'.
! 3. Forward the end-pointer and base arguments.

! 0x8AC–0x8D4: Output a null-terminated string
! Steps:
! 1. Create a stack frame.
! 2. Load the first character.
! 3. Stop when the null terminator is reached.

! 5. Advance the pointer and repeat.

! 0x8D8–0x900: Output a string and append a newline
! Steps:
! 1. Create a stack frame.
! 2. Read the first byte of the input string.
! 3. If the first byte is ASCII 8, print the remaining string.

! 0x904–0x930: Output one hexadecimal digit
! Steps:
! 1. Extract the lowest four bits (one nibble).
! 2. Convert values 0–9 to ASCII '0'–'9'.
! 3. Convert values 10–15 to ASCII 'a'–'f'.
! 4. Send the resulting character to 0x998.
! 5. Return to the caller.

904: 9d e3 bf 98    save  %sp, -104, %sp      ! Create stack frame
908: 90 0e 20 0f    and   %i0, 0xf, %o0      ! Extract low nibble
90c: 80 a2 20 09    cmp   %o0, 9            ! Compare nibble with 9
910: 18 80 00 05    bgu   0x924             ! Branch if value > 9
914: 01 00 00 00    nop                      ! Delay slot

918: 40 00 00 20    call  0x998             ! Output decimal hex digit
91c: 90 12 20 30    or    %o0, 0x30, %o0   ! Delay slot: convert to '0'–'9'
920: 30 80 00 03    b,a   0x92c             ! Unconditional branch, annul slot

924: 40 00 00 1d    call  0x998             ! Output alphabetic hex digit
928: 90 02 20 57    add   %o0, 0x57, %o0   ! Delay slot: convert to 'a'–'f'

92c: 81 c7 e0 08    ret                      ! Return
930: 81 e8 00 00    restore                  ! Delay slot

! 0x934–0x94C: Output an 8-bit value as two hexadecimal digits
! Steps:
! 1. Shift the input right by four bits.
! 2. Output the high nibble.
! 3. Output the low nibble.
! 4. Return.

934: 9d e3 bf 98    save  %sp, -104, %sp
938: 7f ff ff f3    call  0x904             ! Output high nibble
93c: 91 36 20 04    srl   %i0, 4, %o0     ! Delay slot: input >> 4
940: 7f ff ff f1    call  0x904             ! Output low nibble
944: 90 10 00 18    mov   %i0, %o0         ! Delay slot: original input
948: 81 c7 e0 08    ret
94c: 81 e8 00 00    restore

! 0x950–0x970: Output a 16-bit value as four hexadecimal digits
! Steps:
! 1. Start at bit position 12.
! 2. Extract and output one nibble.
! 3. Decrease shift by four bits.
! 4. Repeat through bit position zero.
! 5. Return.

950: 9d e3 bf 98    save  %sp, -104, %sp
954: a0 10 20 0c    mov   0xc, %l0          ! Shift = 12

958: 7f ff ff eb    call  0x904             ! Output nibble
95c: 91 36 00 10    srl   %i0, %l0, %o0   ! Delay slot: extract nibble
960: a0 84 3f fc    addcc %l0, -4, %l0     ! Shift -= 4
964: 1c bf ff fd    bpos  0x958            ! Repeat while shift >= 0
968: 01 00 00 00    nop

96c: 81 c7 e0 08    ret
970: 81 e8 00 00    restore

! 0x974–0x994: Output a 32-bit value as eight hexadecimal digits
! Steps:
! 1. Start at bit position 28.
! 2. Extract and output each nibble from most to least significant.
! 3. Decrease shift by four bits.
! 4. Stop after all eight digits.
! 5. Return.

974: 9d e3 bf 98    save  %sp, -104, %sp
978: a0 10 20 1c    mov   0x1c, %l0         ! Shift = 28

97c: 7f ff ff e2    call  0x904             ! Output nibble
980: 91 36 00 10    srl   %i0, %l0, %o0   ! Delay slot: extract nibble
984: a0 84 3f fc    addcc %l0, -4, %l0     ! Shift -= 4
988: 1c bf ff fd    bpos  0x97c            ! Repeat while shift >= 0
98c: 01 00 00 00    nop

990: 81 c7 e0 08    ret
994: 81 e8 00 00    restore

! 0x998–0x9F0: Polled character output with LF-to-CRLF conversion
! Steps:
! 1. Extract the lowest eight bits of the character.
! 2. Check whether the character is LF (0x0A).
! 3. If LF, wait for transmitter readiness and output CR (0x0D).
! 4. Wait for transmitter readiness again.
! 5. Write the original character to the data register.
! 6. Return.

! 0x998–0x9A4: Check for line feed
998: 84 0a 20 ff    and   %o0, 0xff, %g2
99c: 80 a0 a0 0a    cmp   %g2, 0xa
9a0: 12 80 00 0b    bne   0x9cc
9a4: 05 08 00 00    sethi %hi(0x20000000), %g2

! 0x9A8–0x9C8: Output carriage return before line feed
9a8: 86 10 a0 64    or    %g2, 0x64, %g3       ! Status register address
9ac: c4 00 c0 00    ld    [%g3], %g2           ! Read status
9b0: 80 88 a0 04    btst  4, %g2              ! Test status bit 2
9b4: 02 bf ff fe    be    0x9ac               ! Wait until bit 2 is set
9b8: 05 08 00 00    sethi %hi(0x20000000), %g2 ! Delay slot
9bc: 84 10 a0 60    or    %g2, 0x60, %g2       ! Data register address
9c0: 86 10 20 0d    mov   0xd, %g3             ! ASCII CR
9c4: c6 20 80 00    st    %g3, [%g2]           ! Write CR
9c8: 05 08 00 00    sethi %hi(0x20000000), %g2

! 0x9CC–0x9E8: Output original character
9cc: 86 10 a0 64    or    %g2, 0x64, %g3       ! Status register address
9d0: 90 0a 20 ff    and   %o0, 0xff, %o0       ! Keep lowest byte
9d4: c4 00 c0 00    ld    [%g3], %g2           ! Read status
9d8: 80 88 a0 04    btst  4, %g2              ! Test status bit 2
9dc: 02 bf ff fe    be    0x9d4               ! Wait until ready
9e0: 05 08 00 00    sethi %hi(0x20000000), %g2 ! Delay slot
9e4: 84 10 a0 60    or    %g2, 0x60, %g2       ! Data register address
9e8: d0 20 80 00    st    %o0, [%g2]           ! Write character

! 0x9EC–0x9F0: Return
9ec: 81 c3 e0 08    retl
9f0: 01 00 00 00    nop

! 0x9F4–0xA14: Erase one character on a terminal
! Steps:
! 1. Send backspace (0x08).
! 2. Send a space (0x20).
! 3. Send backspace again.
! 4. Return.

9f4: 9d e3 bf 98    save  %sp, -104, %sp
9f8: 7f ff ff e8    call  0x998
9fc: 90 10 20 08    mov   8, %o0
a00: 7f ff ff e6    call  0x998
a04: 90 10 20 20    mov   0x20, %o0
a08: 7f ff ff e4    call  0x998
a0c: 90 10 20 08    mov   8, %o0
a10: 81 c7 e0 08    ret
a14: 81 e8 00 00    restore

! 0xA18–0xA78: Poll serial-interface status bits
! Steps:
! 1. Initialize MMIO base and a counter limit.
! 2. Wait until status bit 2 is set.
! 3. Wait until status bit 7 is set.
! 4. Wait until status bit 1 is set.
! 5. Repeat until the counter exceeds 0x1387.
! 6. Return.

! 0xA18–0xA30: Initialize addresses and counter
a18: 07 08 00 00    sethi %hi(0x20000000), %g3
a1c: 05 00 00 04    sethi %hi(0x1000), %g2
a20: 94 10 00 03    mov   %g3, %o2
a24: 96 10 a3 87    or    %g2, 0x387, %o3      ! Limit = 0x1387
a28: 86 10 e0 64    or    %g3, 0x64, %g3      ! Status address
a2c: 84 10 20 00    clr   %g2
a30: 92 00 a0 01    add   %g2, 1, %o1        ! Counter = 1

! 0xA34–0xA40: Wait for bit 2
a34: c4 00 c0 00    ld    [%g3], %g2
a38: 80 88 a0 04    btst  4, %g2
a3c: 02 bf ff fe    be    0xa34
a40: 90 12 a0 64    or    %o2, 0x64, %o0

! 0xA44–0xA50: Wait for bit 7
a44: c4 02 00 00    ld    [%o0], %g2
a48: 80 88 a0 80    btst  0x80, %g2
a4c: 02 bf ff fe    be    0xa44
a50: 01 00 00 00    nop

! 0xA54–0xA64: Wait for bit 1
a54: 90 12 a0 64    or    %o2, 0x64, %o0
a58: c4 02 00 00    ld    [%o0], %g2
a5c: 80 88 a0 02    btst  2, %g2
a60: 02 bf ff fe    be    0xa58
a64: 84 10 00 09    mov   %o1, %g2

! 0xA68–0xA78: Check repetition count
a68: 80 a0 80 0b    cmp   %g2, %o3
a6c: 04 bf ff f2    ble   0xa34
a70: 92 00 a0 01    add   %g2, 1, %o1
a74: 81 c3 e0 08    retl
a78: 01 00 00 00    nop

! 0xA7C–0xA94: Initialize argument parsing
! Steps:
! 1. Clear the argument counter.
! 2. Check whether the maximum argument index is exceeded.
! 3. Load the current input character.
! 4. Remember the current input position.

a7c: 86 10 20 00    clr   %g3              ! Argument count = 0
a80: 80 a0 c0 0a    cmp   %g3, %o2         ! Compare count with limit
a84: 34 80 00 27    bg,a  0xb20            ! Return -1 if count > limit
a88: 90 10 3f ff    mov   -1, %o0          ! Annulled delay slot if not taken
a8c: c4 4a 00 00    ldsb  [%o0], %g2       ! Read current character
a90: 10 80 00 03    b     0xa9c            ! Enter whitespace check
a94: 96 10 00 08    mov   %o0, %o3         ! Save current pointer

! 0xA98–0xAB8: Skip spaces and horizontal tabs
! Steps:
! 1. Read the current character.
! 2. Stop on NUL.
! 3. Advance past ASCII space or tab.
! 4. Stop at the first non-whitespace character.

a98: c4 4a 00 00    ldsb  [%o0], %g2
a9c: 80 a0 a0 00    cmp   %g2, 0
aa0: 02 80 00 07    be    0xabc
aa4: 80 a0 a0 20    cmp   %g2, 0x20
aa8: 22 bf ff fc    be,a  0xa98
aac: 90 02 20 01    inc   %o0
ab0: 80 a0 a0 09    cmp   %g2, 9
ab4: 22 bf ff f9    be,a  0xa98
ab8: 90 02 20 01    inc   %o0

! 0xABC–0xAD8: Terminate previous token and check for end of input
! Steps:
! 1. Check whether a previous token exists.
! 2. Replace its separator with NUL.
! 3. Check whether the current character is NUL.
! 4. Return the count when no more tokens remain.

abc: 80 a0 e0 00    cmp   %g3, 0
ac0: 02 80 00 03    be    0xacc
ac4: c4 0a 00 00    ldub  [%o0], %g2
ac8: c0 2a c0 00    clrb  [%o3]
acc: 85 28 a0 18    sll   %g2, 0x18, %g2
ad0: 80 a0 a0 00    cmp   %g2, 0
ad4: 02 80 00 12    be    0xb1c
ad8: 85 28 e0 02    sll   %g3, 2, %g2

! 0xADC–0xAE8: Store the next argument pointer
! Steps:
! 1. Calculate argument index multiplied by four.
! 2. Store the current string pointer.
! 3. Increment argument count.

adc: d0 22 40 02    st    %o0, [%o1 + %g2] ! argv[argc] = p
ae0: c4 4a 00 00    ldsb  [%o0], %g2       ! Read first token character
ae4: 10 80 00 03    b     0xaf0
ae8: 86 00 e0 01    inc   %g3              ! argc++

! 0xAEC–0xB08: Scan to the end of the current token
! Steps:
! 1. Read the next character.
! 2. Stop on NUL, space, or tab.
! 3. Otherwise advance and repeat.

aec: c4 4a 00 00    ldsb  [%o0], %g2
af0: 80 a0 a0 00    cmp   %g2, 0
af4: 02 80 00 06    be    0xb0c
af8: 80 a0 a0 20    cmp   %g2, 0x20
afc: 02 80 00 04    be    0xb0c
b00: 80 a0 a0 09    cmp   %g2, 9
b04: 32 bf ff fa    bne,a  0xaec
b08: 90 02 20 01    inc   %o0


! 0xB0C–0xB24: Continue parsing or return argument count
! Steps:
! 1. Check whether the token ended at NUL.
! 2. If not, repeat the outer loop.
! 3. Otherwise return the number of arguments.

b0c: c4 4a 00 00    ldsb  [%o0], %g2
b10: 80 a0 a0 00    cmp   %g2, 0
b14: 12 bf ff dc    bne   0xa84
b18: 80 a0 c0 0a    cmp   %g3, %o2         ! Delay slot: compare count with limit
b1c: 90 10 00 03    mov   %g3, %o0         ! Return argument count
b20: 81 c3 e0 08    retl
b24: 01 00 00 00    nop
